## Ignored Defaults

### Ignored Directories

`.coverage`, `.dart_tool`, `.env`, `.git`, `.gradle`, `.hg`, `.idea`, `.mypy_cache`, `.next`, `.nuxt`, `.pytest_cache`, `.ruff_cache`, `.svn`, `.turbo`, `.venv`, `.vscode`, `DerivedData`, `Pods`, `__pycache__`, `bin`, `build`, `coverage`, `dist`, `env`, `node_modules`, `obj`, `out`, `target`, `vendor`, `venv`


### Ignored Files

`.DS_Store`, `Cargo.lock`, `Thumbs.db`, `package-lock.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `pubspec.lock`, `yarn.lock`


## Notes

- Binary and generated dependency folders are ignored automatically.
- Comment counting is approximate and based on common comment prefixes.
- Use `--json` to generate machine-readable output beside the markdown report.
- Use `--ignore-dir` and `--ignore-file` to customize ignored paths per project.
